<h2>
<a href="/news/new-review-questions-statin-benefit-lower-risk-primary-prevention-patients" rel="bookmark"><span class="field field--name-title field--type-string field--label-hidden">New Review Questions Statin Benefit in Lower-Risk Primary Prevention Patients </span>
</a>
</h2>
<div class="field field--name-field-teaser field--type-string-long field--label-visually_hidden">
<!-- <div class="field__label visually-hidden">Teaser</div> -->
<div class="field__item">(UPDATED)  A provocative new paper argues that statins may be “low-value care” in this low-risk group, fueling fresh debate. </div>
</div>
</div>

<h2>
<a href="/news/nighttime-hypertension-drug-dosing-may-reduce-risk-cvd" rel="bookmark"><span class="field field--name-title field--type-string field--label-hidden">Nighttime Hypertension Drug Dosing May Reduce Risk of CVD</span>
</a>
</h2>
<div class="field field--name-field-teaser field--type-string-long field--label-hidden field__item">Despite showing a big decrease in risk, the study needs to be reproduced before this strategy is widely adopted, an expert says.</div>
<div class="field field--name-field-author field--type-entity-reference field--label-hidden">
<img class="node-meta__head-shot" src="https://s3.amazonaws.com/prod.tctmd.com/public/2016-10/L.A.%20McKeown.jpg"/>
<span class="node-meta__text">
<span class="by">By</span>
<a href="/staff/la-mckeown" rel="bookmark" title="L.A. McKeown">
    L.A. McKeown
  </a>
</span>
</div>
<div class="field field--name-field-date field--type-datetime field--label-hidden field__item"><time class="datetime" datetime="2019-10-23T19:19:00Z">October 23, 2019</time>
</div>
</div>
<div class="right-container">
<div class="field field--name-field-teaser-image field--type-image field--label-hidden field__item"> <a href="/news/nighttime-hypertension-drug-dosing-may-reduce-risk-cvd" hreflang="en"><img alt="Nighttime Hypertension Drug Dosing May Reduce Risk of CVD" class="image-style-large-teaser" height="202" src="https://s3.amazonaws.com/prod.tctmd.com/public/styles/large_teaser/public/2019-10/nighttime650_0.jpg?itok=vxXG39YT" typeof="foaf:Image" width="343"/>
</a>
</div>
</div>
</article>

<div class="field__item">
<article about="/news/cardiac-rehab-tied-fewer-hospitalizations-deaths-after-valve-surgery" class="has-teaser-image large-news-teaser" data-history-node-id="33641" id="node-33641" role="article">
<div class="left-container">
<div class="pre-title">
<div class="field field--name-field-subtype"><a href="/news">News</a></div>
</div>
<h2>
<a href="/news/cardiac-rehab-tied-fewer-hospitalizations-deaths-after-valve-surgery" rel="bookmark"><span class="field field--name-title field--type-string field--label-hidden">Cardiac Rehab Tied to Fewer Hospitalizations, Deaths After Valve Surgery</span>
</a>
</h2>
<div class="field field--name-field-teaser field--type-string-long field--label-hidden field__item">Fewer than half of the Medicare patients enrolled in rehab programs, indicating much room for improvement.</div>
<div class="field field--name-field-author field--type-entity-reference field--label-hidden">
<img class="node-meta__head-shot" src="https://s3.amazonaws.com/prod.tctmd.com/public/2016-11/Todd%20Neale%20Headshot.jpg"/>
